\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\lesssim}}\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{x}}\left\|\mathcal{F}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{x}}]\ast\tilde{q}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(b)}}{{\leq}}\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{x}}\left\|\mathcal{F}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{x}}]\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\cdot\|\tilde{q}\|^{2}_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(c)}}{{\leq}}\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{x}}\left\|\mathcal{F}[M_{\mathfrak{K},\bm{-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}}]\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}\left(\tilde{I}_{{\bm{\ell}}_{x}}\right)}\cdot\|\tilde{q}\|^{2}_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathcal{F}[M_{\mathfrak{K},\bm{-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}}]\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\cdot\|\tilde{q}\|^{2}_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(d)}}{{=}}\left\|M_{\mathfrak{K},\bm{-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\cdot\|\tilde{q}\|^{2}_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(e)}}{{=}}2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}d_{x}}{s_{x}}}\left\|M_{0,\bm{-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\|\tilde{q}\|^{2}_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}d_{x}}{s_{x}}}.

In the above chain of derivations,
(a)
holds because
\mathcal{F}[\Omega_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{x}}]\ast\tilde{q}
have disjoint support for different
{\bm{\ell}}_{x}
proved in Eq. (
E.2
);
(b)
holds by Young’s convolution inequality;
(c)
holds by Eq. (
105
);
(d)
holds by the fact that
I_{{\bm{\ell}}_{x}}
are pairwise disjoint and Plancherel’s Theorem and
(e)
holds by change of variables
\mathbf{x}\leftarrow 2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{x}}}\mathbf{x}
.
Therefore, combining the upper bound on the above two terms, we obtain

\displaystyle\|f_{\mathbf{v}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(P_{XO})}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}2^{-2\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}\left(1-\frac{d_{x}}{2s_{x}}\right)}2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}d_{x}}{s_{x}}}=\epsilon_{0}^{2}2^{-2\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}.

(126)

We plug the upper bound on
\|f_{\mathbf{v}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(P_{XO})}
back to Eq. (
E.2
) to obtain

\displaystyle\operatorname{KL}\left(P_{f_{\mathbf{v}}},P_{f_{\bm{0}}}\right)\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}\sigma^{-2}2^{-2\mathfrak{K}\frac{\underline{s}d_{x}}{s_{x}}\eta_{1}-2\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}.

(127)

And thus,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|}\sum_{\mathbf{v}\in V_{\mathfrak{K}}}\operatorname{KL}\left(P_{f_{\mathbf{v}}}^{\otimes n},P_{f_{\bm{0}}}^{\otimes n}\right)\leq\epsilon_{0}^{2}n\sigma^{-2}2^{-2\mathfrak{K}\frac{\underline{s}d_{x}}{s_{x}}\eta_{1}-2\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}.

(128)

Step Six

In this step, we are going to show that, for any measurable learning method
(\mathbf{z}_{i},\mathbf{o}_{i},y_{i})_{i=1}^{n}=:D\mapsto\hat{f}_{D}
, there is a distribution
P
among
P_{f_{\mathbf{v}}}
with
\mathbf{v}\in V_{\mathfrak{K}}
which is difficult to learn for the considered learning method. We define a measurable mapping

\displaystyle\Psi:\left([0,1]^{d_{z}}\times[0,1]^{d_{o}}\times\mathbb{R}\right)^{n}\rightarrow V_{\mathfrak{K}},\quad\Psi(D):=\underset{\mathbf{v}\in V_{\mathfrak{K}}}{\operatorname{argmin}}\left\|\hat{f}_{D}-f_{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{L^{2}(P_{XO})}.

(129)

For
\mathbf{v}\in V_{\mathfrak{K}}
and
D\in\left([0,1]^{d_{z}}\times[0,1]^{d_{o}}\times\mathbb{R}\right)^{n}
with
\Psi(D)\neq\mathbf{v}
, we start from (
E.2
) to have

\displaystyle\epsilon_{0}2^{-\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}\zeta^{-\frac{d_{x}}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|f_{\Psi(D)}-f_{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{L^{2}(P_{XO})}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|f_{\Psi(D)}-\hat{f}_{D}\right\|_{L^{2}(P_{XO})}+\left\|\hat{f}_{D}-f_{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{L^{2}(P_{XO})}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\hat{f}_{D}-f_{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{L^{2}(P_{XO})}.

Consequently, for all
\mathbf{v}\in V_{\mathfrak{K}}
we find

\displaystyle P_{f_{\mathbf{v}}}^{\otimes n}(D:\Psi(D)\neq\mathbf{v})\leq P_{f_{\mathbf{v}}}^{\otimes n}\left(D:\left\|\hat{f}_{D}-f_{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{L^{2}(P_{XO})}\gtrsim\epsilon_{0}2^{-\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}\zeta^{-\frac{d_{x}}{2}}\right).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\max_{\mathbf{v}\in V_{\mathfrak{K}}}P_{f_{\mathbf{v}}}^{\otimes n}\left(D:\left\|\hat{f}_{D}-f_{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{L^{2}(P_{XO})}\gtrsim\epsilon_{0}2^{-\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}\zeta^{-\frac{d_{x}}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\max_{\mathbf{v}\in V_{\mathfrak{K}}}P_{f_{\mathbf{v}}}^{\otimes n}(D:\Psi(D)\neq\mathbf{v})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\gtrsim}}

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|}}{\sqrt{|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|}+1}\left(1-\frac{\epsilon_{0}^{2}n2^{-2\mathfrak{K}\frac{\underline{s}d_{x}}{s_{x}}\eta_{1}}2^{-2\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}}{\log|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|}-\frac{1}{2\log|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(b)}}{{\geq}}

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|}}{\sqrt{|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|}+1}\left(1-\frac{\epsilon_{0}^{2}n2^{-2\mathfrak{K}\frac{\underline{s}d_{x}}{s_{x}}\eta_{1}}2^{-2\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}}{\zeta^{-d_{x}}2^{\mathfrak{K}\frac{\underline{s}d_{x}}{s_{x}}+\mathfrak{K}\frac{\underline{s}d_{o}}{s_{o}}}}-\frac{1}{2\log|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|}}{\sqrt{|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|}+1}\left(1-\epsilon_{0}^{2}\zeta^{d_{x}}n2^{-\mathfrak{K}\frac{\underline{s}}{s_{x}}\left(2d_{x}\eta_{1}+2s_{x}+d_{x}+\frac{d_{o}}{s_{o}}s_{x}\right)}-\frac{1}{2\log|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|}\right).

(130)

(a)
holds by
Fischer and Steinwart 2020
and Eq. (
128
).
(b)
holds by the construction of
V_{\mathfrak{K}}
in Eq. (
109
) with
|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|\geq 2^{\frac{\mathcal{L}}{8}}
so
\log|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|\gtrsim\zeta^{-d_{x}}2^{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}(\frac{d_{x}}{s_{x}}+\frac{d_{o}}{s_{o}})}
. Next, we choose
\zeta
and
k
as the following function of
n
:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \zeta=\left(\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{2d_{x}\eta_{1}+2s_{x}+d_{x}+\frac{d_{o}}{s_{o}}s_{x}}\log n\right)^{2},\quad 2^{-\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}=n^{-\frac{s_{x}}{2d_{x}\eta_{1}+2s_{x}+d_{x}+\frac{d_{o}}{s_{o}}s_{x}}}\zeta^{-s_{x}}.\end{aligned}

(131)

The choice of
\zeta
ensures that Eq. (
122
) is satisfied for sufficiently large
n\geq 1
.
The choice of
k
as a function of
n
ensures that we can proceed from Eq. (
130
) to obtain

\displaystyle\quad\max_{\mathbf{v}\in V_{\mathfrak{K}}}P_{f_{\mathbf{v}}}^{\otimes n}\left(D:\left\|\hat{f}_{D}-f_{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{L^{2}(P_{XO})}\geq\epsilon_{0}n^{-\frac{s_{x}}{2s_{x}+2d_{x}\eta_{1}+d_{x}+\frac{d_{o}}{s_{o}}s_{x}}}(\log n)^{-2s_{x}-d_{x}}\right)

\displaystyle\gtrsim\frac{\sqrt{|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|}}{\sqrt{|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|}+1}\left(1-\epsilon_{0}^{2}-\frac{1}{2\log|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-2\epsilon_{0}^{2}.

The last inequality holds for sufficiently large
n
because
|V_{\mathfrak{K}}|\to\infty
as
n\to\infty
.
We set
2\epsilon_{0}^{2}=\tau^{2}
and relabelling the constants, to obtain

\displaystyle\inf_{D\mapsto\hat{f}_{D}}\sup_{f\in B_{2,q}^{s_{x},s_{o}}\left(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}+d_{o}}\right)}\left\|\hat{f}_{D}-f\right\|_{L^{2}(P_{XO})}

\displaystyle\geq\inf_{D\mapsto\hat{f}_{D}}\max_{\mathbf{v}\in V_{\mathfrak{K}}}\left\|\hat{f}_{D}-f_{\mathbf{v}}\right\|_{L^{2}(P_{XO})}

\displaystyle\geq\tau n^{-\frac{s_{x}}{2s_{x}+2d_{x}\eta_{1}+d_{x}+\frac{d_{o}}{s_{o}}s_{x}}}(\log n)^{-2s_{x}-d_{x}}

\displaystyle=\tau n^{-\frac{\frac{s_{x}}{d_{x}}}{1+2(\frac{s_{x}}{d_{x}}+\eta_{1})+\frac{d_{o}}{s_{o}}\frac{s_{x}}{d_{x}}}}(\log n)^{-2s_{x}-d_{x}},

holds for sufficiently large
n
with probability
\geq 1-C\tau^{2}
.

E.3
Auxiliary Results

Lemma E.2
.

Let
f:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}
,
f\in B_{2,q}^{\bm{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and
K\in L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
, then

\displaystyle\|K\ast f\|_{B_{2,q}^{\bm{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\leq\|K\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|f\|_{B_{2,q}^{\bm{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

Proof.

Define
(\tau_{\mathbf{h}}f)(\mathbf{x}):=f(\mathbf{x}+\mathbf{h})
. We have

\displaystyle\tau_{\mathbf{h}}(K\ast f)(\mathbf{x})=(K\ast f)(\mathbf{x}+\mathbf{h})=(K\ast(\tau_{\mathbf{h}}f))(\mathbf{x}).

Since
\Delta^{r}_{\mathbf{h}}=(\tau_{\mathbf{h}}-\mathrm{id})^{r}
, we have
\Delta^{r}_{\mathbf{h}}(K\ast f)=K\ast\left(\Delta^{r}_{\mathbf{h}}f\right)
. Thus for any
r>\max\{s_{1},\ldots,s_{d}\}
,

\displaystyle\omega_{r,2}\left(K\ast f,t^{\frac{1}{s_{1}}},\dots,t^{\frac{1}{s_{d}}},\mathbb{R}^{d}\right)

\displaystyle=\sup_{0<|h_{i}|<t^{\frac{1}{s_{i}}}}\left\|\Delta^{r}_{\mathbf{h}}(K\ast f)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle=\sup_{0<|h_{i}|<t^{\frac{1}{s_{i}}}}\left\|K\ast\Delta^{r}_{\mathbf{h}}(f)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{\leq}}\sup_{0<|h_{i}|<t^{\frac{1}{s_{i}}}}\|K\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\left\|\Delta^{r}_{\mathbf{h}}f\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

(132)

\displaystyle=\|K\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\omega_{r,2}\left(f,t^{\frac{1}{s_{1}}},\dots,t^{\frac{1}{s_{d}}},\mathbb{R}^{d}\right),

where we use Young’s convolution inequality in
(i)
. Hence

\displaystyle|K\ast f|_{B_{2,q}^{\bm{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle=\left(\int_{0}^{1}\left[t^{-1}\omega_{r,2}\left(K\ast f,t^{\frac{1}{s_{1}}},\dots,t^{\frac{1}{s_{d}}},\mathbb{R}^{d}\right)\right]^{q}\;\frac{\mathrm{d}t}{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}

\displaystyle\leq\|K\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\left(\int_{0}^{1}\left[t^{-1}\omega_{r,2}\left(f,t^{\frac{1}{s_{1}}},\dots,t^{\frac{1}{s_{d}}},\mathbb{R}^{d}\right)\right]^{q}\;\frac{\mathrm{d}t}{t}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}

\displaystyle=\|K\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}|f|_{B^{\bm{s}}_{2,q}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

Hence

\displaystyle\|K\ast f\|_{B^{\bm{s}}_{2,q}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle=\|K\ast f\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+|K\ast f|_{B^{\bm{s}}_{2,q}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{\leq}}\|K\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|f\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|K\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}|f|_{B^{\bm{s}}_{2,q}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}=\|K\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|f\|_{B^{\bm{s}}_{2,q}(\mathbb{R}^{d})},

where
(i)
is due to Young’s convolution inequality.
∎

Lemma E.3
.

Let
M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}:\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}\to\mathbb{R},M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}:\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}}\to\mathbb{R}
be defined in Eq. (
98
).
Let
\mathbbm{1}_{S({\bm{\ell}}_{o})}:\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}\to\mathbb{R}
be an indicator function over a measurable set
S({\bm{\ell}}_{o})\subset\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}
for each
{\bm{\ell}}_{o}
in Eq. (
99
).
Define
f(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{o}):=\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}(M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}\ast\mathcal{F}^{-1}[\mathbbm{1}_{S({\bm{\ell}}_{o})}])(\mathbf{x})\cdot M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}(\mathbf{o})
. Then we have

\|f\|_{B^{s_{x},s_{o}}_{2,\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}+d_{o}})}\lesssim 2^{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}-\mathfrak{K}\frac{\underline{s}}{s_{x}}\frac{d_{x}}{2}}
.

Proof.

By definition of Besov norm in
Leisner 2003
[Eq. (2.2)], for any
r>\max\{s_{x},s_{o}\}
, we have

\displaystyle\|f\|_{B^{s_{x},s_{o}}_{2,\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}+d_{o}})}

\displaystyle\asymp\underbrace{\sup_{0<t<1}\left[t^{-1}\omega^{\mathbf{x}}_{r,2}(f,t^{1/s_{x}},\dots,t^{1/s_{x}})\right]}_{(I)}+\underbrace{\sup_{0<t<1}\left[t^{-1}\omega^{\mathbf{o}}_{r,2}(f,t^{1/s_{o}},\dots,t^{1/s_{o}})\right]}_{(II)},

where
\omega_{r,2}^{\mathbf{x}}
(respectively
\omega_{r,2}^{\mathbf{o}}
) denotes the
partial
modulus of smoothness in the
\mathbf{x}
(respectively
\mathbf{o}
)-direction, and we restrict the supremum from
t\in(0,\infty)
to
t\in(0,1)
by
DeVore and Lorentz 1993
. Specifically, define
(\tau_{\mathbf{h}}g)(\mathbf{x}):=g(\mathbf{x}+\mathbf{h})
and
\Delta^{r}_{\mathbf{h}}:=(\tau_{\mathbf{h}}-\mathrm{id})^{r}
, we write

\displaystyle\omega^{\mathbf{x}}_{r,2}(f,t^{1/s_{x}},\dots,t^{1/s_{x}}):=\sup_{|h_{i}|\leq t^{1/s_{x}}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}}\left|\Delta^{r}_{\bm{h}}(f(\cdot,\mathbf{o}))(\mathbf{x})\right|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}\;\mathrm{d}\mathbf{o}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

and
\omega_{r,2}^{\mathbf{o}}
is defined similarly. We first bound
(I)
. We have

\displaystyle\tau_{\mathbf{h}}(K\ast g)(\mathbf{x})=(K\ast g)(\mathbf{x}+\mathbf{h})=(K\ast(\tau_{\mathbf{h}}g))(\mathbf{x}).

We thus have
\Delta^{r}_{\mathbf{h}}(K\ast g)=K\ast\left(\Delta^{r}_{\mathbf{h}}g\right)
. Thus we have

\displaystyle\omega^{\mathbf{x}}_{r,2}(f,t^{1/s_{x}},\dots,t^{1/s_{x}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{|h_{i}|\leq t^{1/s_{x}}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}}\left|\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}((\Delta_{\bm{h}}^{r}M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}})\ast\mathcal{F}^{-1}[\mathbbm{1}_{S({\bm{\ell}}_{o})}])(\mathbf{x})M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}(\mathbf{o})\right|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}\;\mathrm{d}\mathbf{o}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{|h_{i}|\leq t^{1/s_{x}}}\left(\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}^{\prime}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}}((\Delta_{\bm{h}}^{r}M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}})\ast\mathcal{F}^{-1}[\mathbbm{1}_{S({\bm{\ell}}_{o})}])(\mathbf{x})\overline{((\Delta_{\bm{h}}^{r}M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}})\ast\mathcal{F}^{-1}[\mathbbm{1}_{S({\bm{\ell}}_{o}^{\prime})}])(\mathbf{x})}\;\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}(\mathbf{o})M_{k,{\bm{\ell}}_{o}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{o})\;\mathrm{d}\mathbf{o}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\sup_{|h_{i}|\leq t^{1/s_{x}}}\left(\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}[\mathbbm{1}_{S({\bm{\ell}}_{o})}]\ast(\Delta^{r}_{\bm{h}}M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}^{2}\|M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}})}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(b)}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\sup_{|h_{i}|\leq t^{1/s_{x}}}\left(\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\|\Delta^{r}_{\bm{h}}M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}^{2}\|M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}})}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\sup_{|h_{i}|\leq t^{1/s_{x}}}\|\Delta^{r}_{\bm{h}}M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\right)\left(\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\|M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}})}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(c)}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\sup_{|h_{i}|\leq t^{1/s_{x}}}\|\Delta^{r}_{\bm{h}}M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\omega_{r,2}(M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}},t^{1/s_{x}},\dots,t^{1/s_{x}}).

In the above derivations,

•

we use
\mathrm{supp}(M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}})\cap\mathrm{supp}(M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}})=\emptyset
if
{\bm{\ell}}_{o}\neq{\bm{\ell}}_{o}^{\prime}
in
(a)
,

•

we use in
(b)
the inequality

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\quad\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}[\mathbbm{1}_{S({\bm{\ell}}_{o})}]\ast(\Delta^{r}_{\bm{h}}M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}})\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}=\|\mathbbm{1}_{S({\bm{\ell}}_{o})}\cdot\mathcal{F}[\Delta^{r}_{\bm{h}}M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}]\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\\
&\leq\|\mathcal{F}[\Delta_{\bm{h}}^{r}M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}]\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}=\|\Delta_{\bm{h}}^{r}M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})},\end{aligned}

(133)

•

we use
\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\|M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}})}\leq 2^{\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}d_{o}}{s_{o}}}2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}d_{o}}{s_{o}}}\|M_{0\bm{0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}})}\leq 1
in
(c)
. Indeed, note that
\|M_{0\bm{0}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}})}=\|\iota_{m}\|^{2d_{o}}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\leq\|\iota_{m}\|^{2d_{o}}_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})}=1
.

Thus we have

\displaystyle(I)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t>0}\left[t^{-1}\omega_{r,2}\left(M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}},t^{\frac{1}{s_{x}}},\dots,t^{\frac{1}{s_{x}}}\right)\right]\asymp|M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}|_{B^{s_{x}}_{2,\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\asymp 2^{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}\left(1-\frac{d_{x}}{2s_{x}}\right)},

where we bound
(I)
by an isotropic Besov norm, and we use the sequential Besov norm equivalence
[
DeVore and Popov 1988
]
[Theorem 5.1] (see also
Leisner 2003
applied to
B^{s_{x}}_{2,\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})
) in the last equality. Now we bound
(II)
. Notice that

\displaystyle\omega^{\mathbf{o}}_{r,2}(f,t^{1/s_{o}},\dots,t^{1/s_{o}})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\omega_{s_{o},2}^{\mathbf{o}}(f,t^{1/s_{o}},\dots,t^{1/s_{o}})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(b)}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle 2^{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}\omega^{\mathbf{o}}_{s_{o},2}\left(f,2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{1/s_{o}},\dots,2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{1/s_{o}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}\sup_{|h_{i}|\leq 2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{1/s_{o}}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}+d_{o}}}\left|\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}(\mathcal{F}^{-1}[\mathbbm{1}_{S({\bm{\ell}}_{o})}]\ast M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}})(\mathbf{x})\cdot(\Delta^{s_{o}}_{\bm{h}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}})(\mathbf{o})\right|^{2}\;\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}\;\mathrm{d}\mathbf{o}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}\sup_{|h_{i}|\leq 2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{1/s_{o}}}\left(\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}^{\prime}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}}(\mathcal{F}^{-1}[\mathbbm{1}_{S({\bm{\ell}}_{o})}]\ast M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}})(\mathbf{x})\cdot\overline{\mathcal{F}^{-1}[\mathbbm{1}_{S({\bm{\ell}}_{o}^{\prime})}]\ast M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}}(\mathbf{x})\;\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}}}(\Delta^{s_{o}}_{\bm{h}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}})(\mathbf{o})\cdot(\Delta^{s_{o}}_{\bm{h}}M_{k,{\bm{\ell}}_{o}^{\prime}})(\mathbf{o})\;\mathrm{d}\mathbf{o}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(c)}}{{=}}

\displaystyle 2^{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}\sup_{|h_{i}|\leq 2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{1/s_{o}}}\left(\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}[\mathbbm{1}_{S({\bm{\ell}}_{o})}]\ast M_{k,{\bm{\ell}}_{x}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\cdot\|\Delta^{s_{o}}_{\bm{h}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(d)}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle 2^{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}\sup_{|h_{i}|\leq 2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{1/s_{o}}}\left(\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\|M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\cdot\|\Delta^{s_{o}}_{\bm{h}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}\|M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\left(\sup_{|h_{i}|\leq 2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{1/s_{o}}}\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\|\Delta^{s_{o}}_{\bm{h}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(e)}}{{=}}

\displaystyle 2^{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}\|M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\left(\sup_{|h_{i}|\leq 2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{1/s_{o}}}\left\|\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\left(\Delta^{s_{o}}_{\bm{h}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\right)\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(f)}}{{=}}

\displaystyle 2^{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}\|M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\left(\sup_{|h_{i}|\leq 2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{1/s_{o}}}\left\|\Delta^{s_{o}}_{\bm{h}}\left(\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\right)\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}\|M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\left(\omega_{s_{o},2}\left(\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}},2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{\frac{1}{s_{o}}},\dots,2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{\frac{1}{s_{o}}}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

In the above derivations,

•

we use
r=s_{x}\vee s_{o}\geq s_{o}
and Minkowski’s inequality in
(a)
(this is also sometimes referred to as the reverse Marchaud inequality, see
Kolomoitsev and Tikhonov 2020
[Property 8]),

•

we use
Leisner 2003
[Theorem 2.1.1] in
(b)
, which we can apply since
2^{\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}\geq 1
,

•

we deduce from Eq. (
98
) that

\displaystyle\mathrm{supp}(M_{k{\bm{\ell}}_{o}})=\bigtimes_{j=1}^{d_{o}}\left[2^{-\left\lfloor\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}\right\rfloor}\ell_{o,j},2^{-\left\lfloor\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}\right\rfloor}(\mathfrak{m}+\ell_{o,j})\right].

We also see that

\displaystyle\mathrm{supp}(\Delta^{s_{o}}_{\bm{h}}M_{k{\bm{\ell}}_{o}})=\bigtimes_{j=1}^{d_{o}}\left[2^{-\left\lfloor\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}\right\rfloor}\ell_{o,j}-s_{o}h_{j},2^{-\left\lfloor\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}\right\rfloor}(\mathfrak{m}+\ell_{o,j})\right]

We deduce that a sufficient condition to guarantee that

\displaystyle\mathrm{supp}\left(\Delta^{s_{o}}_{\bm{h}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\right)\cap\mathrm{supp}\left(\Delta^{s_{o}}_{\bm{h}}M_{k,{\bm{\ell}}_{o}^{\prime}}\right)=\emptyset

if
{\bm{\ell}}_{o}\neq{\bm{\ell}}_{o}^{\prime}
is given by

\displaystyle(\forall j=1,\dots,d_{o}),\;|h_{j}|\leq 2^{-\left\lfloor\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}\right\rfloor}\frac{\mathfrak{m}-1}{s_{o}}.

Since
\mathfrak{m}-1\geq s_{o}
by choice, and we have for all
i=1,\dots,d_{o}
,
|h_{i}|\leq 2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{1/s_{o}}\leq 2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}
, this sufficient condition is satisfied. Hence

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta^{s_{o}}_{\bm{h}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}},\Delta^{s_{o}}_{\bm{h}}M_{k,{\bm{\ell}}_{o}^{\prime}}\right\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}})}=\left\|\Delta^{s_{o}}_{\bm{h}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}})}^{2}\delta_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}^{\prime}}.

(134)

•

we use Plancherel’s Theorem in
(d)
, in a similar way as in Eq. (
133
),

•

we use Eq. (
134
) again for step
(e)
,

•

we use linearity of
\Delta^{s_{o}}_{\bm{h}}
for step
(f)
.

Thus we have shown that, for
q=\infty
,

\displaystyle(II)

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}\|M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\sup_{t>0}\left[t^{-1}\omega_{s_{o},2}\left(\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}},2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{\frac{1}{s_{o}}},\dots,2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{\frac{1}{s_{o}}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\|M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\sup_{t>0}\left[\left(t2^{-\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}\right)^{-1}\omega_{s_{o},2}\left(\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}},2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{\frac{1}{s_{o}}},\dots,2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}}t^{\frac{1}{s_{o}}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\lesssim}}\|M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\sup_{t>0}\left[\int_{t}^{\infty}w^{-s_{o}}\omega_{s_{o}+1}\left(\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}},w,\dots,w\right)\;\frac{\mathrm{d}w}{w}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\|M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\int_{0}^{\infty}w^{-s_{o}}\omega_{s_{o}+1}\left(\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}},w,\dots,w\right)\;\frac{\mathrm{d}w}{w}

\displaystyle\asymp\|M_{k,-\frac{\mathfrak{m}}{2}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}})}\left\|\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}M_{\mathfrak{K},{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}\right\|_{B^{s_{o}}_{2,1}(\mathbb{R}^{d_{o}})}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(b)}}{{\lesssim}}2^{-\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}d_{x}}{2s_{x}}}2^{\frac{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}}{s_{o}}(s_{o}-d_{o}/2)}\left(\sum_{{\bm{\ell}}_{o}}1\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\mathfrak{K}\underline{s}\left(1-\frac{d_{x}}{2s_{x}}\right)},

where we use the Marchaud-type estimate
DeVore and Lorentz 1993
in
(a)
, we use the sequential Besov norm equivalence
DeVore and Popov 1988
[Theorem 5.1] (see also
[
Leisner 2003
, Theorem 3.3.3]
) in
(b)
.
∎
